# FIX-154 ƒ?" Implementation Handoff for Code Review

Date: 2026-10-10  
Status: implemented in the local working tree; not deployed.

## Scope

FIX-154 changes only higher-timeframe BUY authority. It does not enable or
change FIX-153 reconciliation/runtime behavior.

The objective is to distinguish trusted, strong bearish 1h evidence from
unknown or weaker evidence without changing a stored historical signal.

## Implemented policy

1. The current 1m BUY still requires fresh 5m and 1h context.
2. A bearish 5m returns the existing `5M_BEARISH_VETO` first.
3. A bearish 1h with `data_quality_valid` `NULL` or `false` returns
   `1H_BEARISH_VETO`.
4. A trusted 1h `STRONG_SELL` with `confidence_score >= 70` returns
   `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`.
5. A trusted 1h `SELL`, or `STRONG_SELL` with confidence below 70, is a
   SOFT conflict. It passes effective 1h `NEUTRAL` to profile logic and caps
   every allowed initial entry at 25%.
6. A non-bearish 1h with unknown quality keeps its raw decision; FIX-154 does
   not tighten bullish/non-bearish authority merely because historical quality
   is absent.
7. Existing progressive/add logic retains its raw 1h requirement. A SOFT
   conflict cannot authorize an add. Optional `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence
   is observational only.

## Database changes

`V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql` adds:

- `trade_signal.data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL`.
- Replay parity columns on `trade_signal_test`:
  `btc_context_candle_close_time`, `btc_context_age_seconds`,
  `btc_context_freshness_threshold_seconds`, `signal_mode`, and
  `data_quality_valid`.
- Append-only `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, including effective/raw decisions,
  candle-close-based age, quality evidence, the hard-confirmation marker,
  cap, legacy veto and final result.

Historical `data_quality_valid` stays `NULL`; no value is backfilled.

## Included production code

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `domain/TradeSignal.java` | Maps nullable `data_quality_valid`. |
| `service/AnalysisService.java` | Persists `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` at signal generation. |
| `service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java` | Pure FIX-154 decision matrix; returns detached effective decisions and never mutates entities. |
| `service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java` | Applies assessment to direct BUY and contextual/accumulated authority; enforces SOFT cap; records non-blocking audit evidence. |
| `service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java` | Applies the same matrix and cap to consolidation. |
| `execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java` | Keeps existing progressive raw-1h guard; invokes optional audit-only SOFT evidence after its rejection. Uses `SETUP_WAKEUP` audit path for the 5m wake-up caller. |
| `service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java` | Snapshots and asynchronously persists best-effort Production audit rows. Replay writes no Production audit row. |
| `config/Fix154AuditAsyncConfig.java` | Dedicated one-thread, bounded executor; audit saturation drops evidence rather than borrowing a trading connection. |
| `service/Fix154Metrics.java` | Non-gating counters for untrusted 1h blocks, audit failures, SOFT caps and SOFT add blocks. |
| `service/Fix154DiagnosticsController.java` | Read-only `GET /api/fix154/health` metrics endpoint. |
| `service/Fix154RuntimeSchemaChecks.java` | Checks V107-required production and Replay schema before runtime admission. |

## Replay behavior

The regular reflection-based Replay writer persists every JPA `TradeSignal`
column. V107 therefore updates `trade_signal_test` alongside production.
FIX-154 audit rows are deliberately skipped while `ExecutionReplayScope` is
active because replay-generated entities do not have Production IDs and parity
is recorded by Replayƒ?Ts own evidence tables.

## Tests included

- `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`
  - hard, soft, untrusted, 5m-veto precedence, 69/70 boundary, BNB #150a and
    #150b policy cases, and unknown non-bearish authority.
- `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`
  - direct/context SOFT caps, conservative behavior, neutral-5m exception is
    not unlocked, audit failure isolation, Production/Replay parity, and
    future-context exclusion.
- `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`
  - SOFT cap through consolidation.
- `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStoreTest`
  - Replay does not write a Production audit row.
- `Fix154MigrationContractTest`
  - V107 contains production, Replay and audit schema contracts.

# FIX-154 ƒ?" Trusted higher-timeframe BUY validation

Status: implementation design approved. FIX-154 is scheduled before the
remaining FIX-153 runtime release work.

## Purpose and boundary

FIX-154 replaces the current binary fresh-1h bearish veto with a trusted
higher-timeframe assessment. It does not relax the existing 5m confirmation
requirement, risk/reward result, current-1m BTC protection, or profile rules
except where a raw 1h decision would otherwise act as an untrusted veto.

The legacy `src/main/resources/md/FIX-146.md` is an unrelated shared-market
readiness design and is not an implementation source for this fix.

## Current integration points to replace

The 1h/5m blocks in all three paths below must be replaced as a whole by one
shared assessment call. Do not add this decision to `FinalDecisionService`.

1. `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuy()` ƒ?" direct 1m BUYs.
2. `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuyContext()` ƒ?" accumulated/setup
   authority, whose reference may be a 5m signal.
3. `OpportunityConsolidationService.evaluate()` ƒ?" consolidated BUYs.

Each replacement owns the current lookup, freshness, 5m veto, and 1h veto
block in that method. The remaining profile/consolidation logic consumes the
assessment's effective context; it must not reread the raw 1h decision as an
independent veto.

## Immutable signal evidence

Migration `V107` adds:

```sql
ALTER TABLE trade_signal ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;
```

`AnalysisService` writes `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` through the
existing `TradeSignal.builder()` call for every newly generated signal on every
interval. Historic rows are never backfilled: `NULL` means unknown/invalid.

FIX-154 does not depend on `signal_mode` or any other FIX-153 runtime/schema
feature. `data_quality_valid = true` is sufficient evidence eligibility for
this release; false or `NULL` is unusable. Historical-only exclusion is added
later with FIX-153, after its own migration/runtime release is approved.

Historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` is audit evidence only. It is never a
FIX-154 usability or hard-veto predicate. The current 1m BTC guard is unchanged.

## Time-bounded selection and fixed freshness

For a request with reference signal `R`, use `R.generatedAt` as `evaluatedAt`.
Select the latest same-symbol 5m and 1h signal such that both its generated
time and its candle close are at or before `evaluatedAt`; no wall-clock lookup
is permitted. Replay supplies the same historical reference time.

Existing fixed freshness limits remain authoritative:

| Interval | Maximum age |
|---|---:|
| 5m | 20 minutes |
| 1h | 3 hours |

`MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` and `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` retain their existing
meaning and priority when their respective interval is missing or stale. A
stale signal is not quietly converted into an allowed BUY just because it is
`NOT_TRUSTED` for FIX-154.

## Usability and 1h strength

A fresh row is usable for FIX-154 only if `data_quality_valid = true`.

FIX-154 v1 does not invent ADX/DMI/VWAP/CMF evidence, because those indicators
are not currently persisted. Its fixed strength definition is:

```text
HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE = 1h decision == STRONG_SELL
                         AND confidence_score >= 70
SOFT_CONFLICT          = fresh, quality-valid 1h decision == SELL
                         OR STRONG_SELL with confidence_score below 70
NOT_TRUSTED            = fresh but quality-invalid/unknown/historical-only 1h
                         OR fresh non-bearish 1h
```

`confidence_score` is the persisted effective confidence. `raw_confidence_score`
is explicitly not used. The constant `70` is a named FIX-154 configuration
default, persisted in the audit row, and may change only through a reviewed
strategy change with Replay regression.

## Exact 1h A- 5m decision matrix

First apply existing missing/stale checks. Only then apply this table.

| 1h state | 5m state | Final HTF result before profile | Profile inputs |
|---|---|---|---|
| missing/stale | any | existing `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` rejection | none |
| any | missing/stale | existing `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` rejection | none |
| NOT_TRUSTED bearish from false/NULL quality | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| NOT_TRUSTED bearish from false/NULL quality | bullish/neutral/watch | `1H_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| NOT_TRUSTED non-bearish from false/NULL quality | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| NOT_TRUSTED non-bearish from false/NULL quality | bullish/neutral/watch | continue to profile | raw 1h, raw 5m |
| SOFT_CONFLICT | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| SOFT_CONFLICT | bullish/neutral/watch | continue to profile, cap resulting first entry at 25% | effective 1h=`NEUTRAL`, raw 5m |
| HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE | bearish, quality-valid | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO`; audit `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT` | none |
| HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE | bullish/neutral/watch, quality-valid | `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| fresh, quality-valid non-bearish 1h | non-bearish | continue to profile | raw 1h, raw 5m |

The profile call produces the final decision for every `continue to profile`
row. It may still return its existing insufficient-confirmation code. It must
not consult the raw 1h row after this mapping.

`5m quality=false/NULL` does not alter the legacy 5m fallback. A fresh raw
bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO`, exactly as today. It merely cannot
act as new FIX-154 hard-veto confirmation or a soft-conflict release.

The veto order is fixed: a bearish 5m returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any
trusted-1h result. If the 1h is hard, audit records `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT`
on that legacy outcome. When 5m is not bearish, a hard trusted 1h returns
`HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`. The only deliberate lasting relaxation is a trusted
SOFT bearish 1h: an otherwise allowed initial entry is capped at 25%.

## Profile contract

`conservativeBuy`, `balancedBuy`, `balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException`, and
`aggressiveBuy` receive the assessment's effective 1h/5m contextƒ?"not raw
selected 1h evidence. A SOFT conflict maps to `NEUTRAL`, never `WATCH`, then
the validation result is capped with `min(profilePositionPercent, 25)`.
This means the neutral-5m/direct-BUY exception still requires a genuinely
bullish 1h and cannot be unlocked by SOFT evidence. An untrusted non-bearish
1h retains its raw decision so deployment does not accidentally tighten current
bullish authority.

The assessment result carries `maxInitialPercent`: `25` for `SOFT_CONFLICT`,
otherwise no cap. Every caller that selects a size must use
`min(itsCalculatedPercent, maxInitialPercent)` when the cap is present. This
includes direct validation, `validateBuyContext()` callers (the 5m-trigger
path and accumulated evidence), and
`OpportunityConsolidationService.consolidatedPositionPercent()`.

The raw selected signals remain audit evidence. Existing 5m requirements inside
profiles remain unchanged. HARD and untrusted-bearish outcomes never call a
profile method.

### Add-on contract

`ExecutionIntelligenceService.progressivePositionDecision()` already reads raw
1h evidence and requires `WATCH` or bullish support for `SCOUT_ENTRY`,
`CONFIRMATION_ADD`, and `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`. Keep that check unchanged and
outside the three replacement paths; it already blocks HARD, SOFT, and
untrusted-bearish 1h states. Do not route it through effective FIX-154 context,
which could widen behavior. When that existing check rejects while the assessed
band is SOFT, record `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` as audit/metric evidence only.

## Append-only execution audit

FIX-154 is evaluated during execution/consolidation, not while a signal is
saved. Migration `V107` creates append-only `buy_htf_assessment_audit`:

- primary key ID;
- reference signal ID, evaluation path (`DIRECT_BUY`, `BUY_CONTEXT`,
  `CONSOLIDATION`, `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`, `CONFIRMATION_ADD`,
  `ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE_ADD`) and `evaluated_at`; no unique constraint;
- raw/effective 1h and 5m signal IDs, decisions, signal modes, candle closes,
  ages, data-quality values, and usability reasons;
- configured confidence threshold, band, legacy-veto result, profile context,
  final result/reason, Production/Replay mode, and creation timestamp.

The audit is written best-effort after the relevant execution/consolidation
decision is known. A duplicate or persistence failure is logged and increments
`fix154.audit_write_failure_count`; it must never roll back, reject, or alter a
trading decision. It is evidence onlyƒ?"not a fence or an input to later trading.

## Deployment behavior

Immediately after deployment all pre-V107 1h/5m rows have
`data_quality_valid=NULL`. They are treated as `NOT_TRUSTED`; they cannot make a
new permissive decision: a bearish unknown 1h remains blocked, while a
non-bearish unknown 1h retains its existing profile authority. The health view
must expose `fix154.untrusted_bearish_1h_block_count` and
`fix154.audit_write_failure_count`, `fix154.soft_capped_entry_count`, and
`fix154.soft_add_blocked_count`, grouped by execution path.

## Migration ordering and FIX-153 independence

The configured local database has V106 as its latest applied Flyway migration.
Therefore FIX-154 reserves `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`.
Before this migration is applied anywhere, rename the currently unreleased
`V107__fix_153_local_candle_reconciliation.sql` to V108. Never rename or edit
an applied migration. Do not enable Flyway `outOfOrder`; deployment order is
made explicit instead.

## Required regression suite

- All three integration paths use the shared service and no longer contain raw
  `1H_BEARISH_VETO` branches.
- `STRONG_SELL` + `confidence_score >= 70` and bearish fresh valid 5m produces
  legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` with `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT` audit; the same 1h
  with non-bearish 5m produces `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`.
- Strong/soft/untrusted 1h plus bullish, neutral, watch, bearish, missing,
  stale, false-quality, and NULL-quality 5m covers every matrix row and its
  final profile result.
- `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` (3h) and `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` (20m) retain their
  priority and codes.
- BNB #150a: persisted historical 1h bearish row with `NULL` quality =>
  `NOT_TRUSTED` and `1H_BEARISH_VETO`.
- BNB #150b: Replay regenerates that same 1h with quality evidence and it is
  SOFT; no trusted hard veto is emitted and the final expected result is
  `REJECTED_RISK_REWARD` when the risk/reward guard fails.
- Historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` does not change usability; current
  1m BTC behavior is unchanged.
- FIX-154 migration succeeds with no `signal_mode` column and its assessment
  has no FIX-153 schema/runtime dependency.
- No future signal/candle is selected in Production or Replay; both produce
  equivalent audit evidence for the same historical inputs.
- Pre-migration `NULL` quality is never treated as valid.
- Repeated execution/consolidation evaluations append audit rows and audit
  failure leaves the business decision intact.
- A SOFT initial entry never exceeds 25% through progressive, confirmation, or
  accumulated-evidence or consolidation sizing; existing progressive raw-1h
  support rules block add stages while the 1h remains bearish.

## Release conditions

Run Java 21 unit/integration tests, migration tests, Production/Replay parity,
and a bounded no-look-ahead historical replay before enabling FIX-154.
FIX-153 remains independent and retains its own release gates.

## Revision addendum ƒ?" Round 5 approval conditions

This addendum governs where it differs from earlier wording.

### Release build decision

The FIX-154 release build includes `V108__fix_153_local_candle_reconciliation.sql`
after the current unreleased FIX-153 V107 migration is renumbered to V108.
This is required even with reconciliation disabled: `candle_analysis_outcome`
is used by ordinary live signal processing, terminal worker outcomes, and the
closed-candle detector. Omitting V108 would cause missing-table SQL failures;
in particular, a normal signal-processing completion would roll back because
its outcome write is inside the processing transaction.

Including V108 ships FIX-153 reconciliation *schema only*. The reconciliation
runtime remains disabled and retains all of its separate release gates.

### No FIX-153 provenance dependency

Ignore earlier references to historical-only status or signal modes in the
FIX-154 band definition and audit payload. FIX-154 v1 eligibility depends only
on time-bounded selection and `data_quality_valid = true`; `signal_mode` is not
read, required, or stored by the FIX-154 audit.

### Effective context is transient

The assessment returns immutable effective decision values separately from the
loaded `TradeSignal` entities. No code may call `setDecision`, mutate, merge,
or save a selected 1h/5m signal to obtain effective `NEUTRAL` context. Profile
methods receive explicit effective decisions (or an immutable assessment
context), while the raw entity remains audit evidence only. A regression test
must load a SOFT 1h signal, execute the assessment/profile path, flush/clear,
and prove its stored decision is unchanged.

### Progressive-path evidence is observational

`progressivePositionDecision()` retains its existing raw-1h support check as
the only decision authority for scout/add paths. If that existing check passes,
FIX-154 performs no assessment lookup and writes no progressive audit row. If
it rejects because raw 1h is bearish, a best-effort audit-only assessment may
record `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` when the band is SOFT. That observation must not
alter the rejection, allow an add, cause a database lookup on passing paths, or
propagate an audit failure into the trading decision.

V108 ships with FIX-154 because the current runtime writes
`candle_analysis_outcome`. Reconciliation remains disabled.

### Round 5 text clarification

This addendum removes any earlier contrary implication: FIX-154 v1 has no
`signal_mode` or historical-only predicate or audit field. Effective 1h/5m
decisions are separate immutable values and never mutate a loaded signal.
Progressive-path assessment is audit-only after its existing raw-1h check has
already rejected; it is skipped entirely when that check passes and never feeds
the scout/add decision.

## Implementation completion record ƒ?" 2026-10-10

The approved FIX-154 design is now implemented in the working tree.

- `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql` creates the nullable signal
  quality evidence and append-only assessment audit table. No historical value
  is fabricated or backfilled.
- `AnalysisService` persists `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new
  signal.
- `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` owns the hard/soft/untrusted decision
  matrix. It returns immutable effective decisions and does not mutate a JPA
  signal.
- Direct BUY validation, accumulated-evidence authority, and opportunity
  consolidation invoke that one service. A SOFT result uses effective 1h
  `NEUTRAL` and applies the 25% initial-position cap. The legacy bearish-5m
  veto retains precedence. Progressive adds retain their existing raw 1h
  requirement and can only create best-effort audit evidence after rejection.
- `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore` is append-only and runs in an independent
  transaction. It catches insert failures so audit availability cannot alter a
  business decision.

Focused Java 21 verification passed:
`HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
`TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
`OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.

This is implementation completion, not production deployment approval. Apply
V107/V108 only through the normal clean Java 21 release pipeline and complete
the required migration, Replay-parity, and broader regression validation before
enabling it in Production.

203:                             "progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",

205:                     lock.progressPercent(), lock.activationPercent(), lock.lockPrice(), lock.state(), lock.triggered());
206:         }
207:         if (lock.triggered()) {
208:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_TRIGGER] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, target={}",
209:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lock.progressPercent(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
210:             BigDecimal hardProfitFloor = managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
211:             if (price.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0) {
212:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " Hard protected-profit floor " + hardProfitFloor + " was breached.";
213:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
214:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
215:                 }
216:                 return;
217:             }
218: 
219:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(one, five, hour);
220:             if (lockDecision.exit()) {
221:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " " + lockDecision.explanation();
222:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, lockDecision.code(), reason)) {
223:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, lockDecision.code(), reason, one);
224:                     log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK exit: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
225:                             symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.code());
226:                 }
227:                 return;
228:             }
229:             log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK protected hold: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
230:                     symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.explanation());
231:         }
232: 
233:         // Normal signal exits remain active even when Profit Lock is disabled. Higher-timeframe
234:         // failure can therefore close a winner instead of waiting forever for an exact 1m SELL event.
235:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(one, five, hour);
236:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
237:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation())) {
238:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation(), one);
239:                 log.info("Live signal-driven exit: symbol={}, price={}, reason={}", symbol, price, normalExit.code());
240:             }
241:             // FIX-11T: any higher-priority position action owns this evaluation cycle.
242:             // Near-TP must never race another SELL or quantity-changing decision on the same tick.
243:             return;
244:         }
245: 
246:         // FIX-11T: Near-TP Failure Protection is deliberately LAST. TP/SL/profit-lock and
247:         // existing signal exits above retain their exact authority and always win the cycle.
248:         evaluateNearTpProtection(managed, price, one, five);
249:     }
250: 
45: 
46:         return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
47:                 + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
48:                 + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
49:                 + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
50:     }
51: 
52:     public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
53:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
54:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
55:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
56: 
57:         // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
58:         if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
59:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
60:                     "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
61:                             + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
62:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
63:                             + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
64:         }
65: 
66:         // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
67:         if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
68:                 && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
69:                 && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
70:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
71:                     "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
72:                             + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
73:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
74:                             + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
75:         }
76: 
77:         if (bearish(oneDecision)) {
78:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_HTF_EXIT",
79:                     "Profit Lock was breached and 1h is " + label(oneDecision)
80:                             + "; higher-timeframe support has failed.");
81:         }
82: 
83:         if (bearish(currentDecision) && bearish(fiveDecision)) {
84:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_BEARISH_CONFIRMATION",
85:                     "Profit Lock was breached with bearish 1m and 5m confirmation.");
86:         }
87: 
88:         return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT",
89:                 "Profit Lock was breached without enough higher-timeframe bullish support to justify a protected hold:"
90:                         + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
91:                         + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
92:                         + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
93:     }
94: 
95:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal signal) {
96:         return signal == null ? null : signal.getDecision();
97:     }
98: 
99:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision decision) {
100:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY

487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
488:             PaperPosition position,
489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
490:             PositionStatus status,
491:             String closeReason,
492:             String explanation,
493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
494:     ) {
495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
500: 
501:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
502:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
503:         position.setStatus(status);
504:         position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
505:         position.setExitReason(explanation);
506:         position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
507:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
508:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
509: 
510:         // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
511:         // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
512:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
513: 
514:         if (exitSignal != null) {
515:             try {
516:                 // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
517:                 // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
518:                 // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
519:                 // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
520:                 if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
521:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
522:                 } else {
523:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
524:                 }
525:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
526:                 log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
527:             }
528:         }
529: 
530:         // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
531:         // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
532:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
533:         return saved;
534:     }

536:     private String normalizeSymbol(String symbol) {
537:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
538:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
539:         }
540:         return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
541:     }

543:     private void enforceDailyLossLimit() {
544:         Instant startOfDay = LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC).atStartOfDay().toInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);
545:         BigDecimal pnl = positionRepository.sumRealizedPnlSince(startOfDay);
546:         BigDecimal maximumLoss = properties.paperAccountBalance()
547:                 .multiply(properties.maxDailyLossPercent(), MC)
548:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
549:                 .negate();
550: 
365:                 // Existing behavior: no earned protection is carried into the new geometry.
366:                 managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.INACTIVE);
367:                 managed.setNearTpBestPrice(null);
368:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
369:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
370:             }
371:             case NEAR_TP_ARMED -> {
372:                 // FIX-117: preserve earned protection and the real historical best price.
373:             }
374:             case NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED -> {
375:                 // Preserve earned protection/best price, but discard bearish persistence
376:                 // accumulated under the old, tighter geometry. The next evaluation will
377:                 // recalculate giveback against the new TP and either recover or remain rejected.
378:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
379:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
380:             }
381:             case NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED -> {
382:                 // Existing terminal behavior; normally returned above by nearTpHarvestUsed.
383:             }
384:         }
385:     }
386: 
387:     private boolean sameDecimal(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
388:         return a == null ? b == null : b != null && a.compareTo(b) == 0;
389:     }
390: 
391:     private void closePaper(String symbol, BigDecimal exitPrice, PositionStatus status,
392:                             String closeReason, String explanation, TradeSignal sourceSignal) {
393:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
394:         if (paper == null) return;
395:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(paper.getEntryPrice(), MC).multiply(paper.getQuantity(), MC);
396:         // FIX-11T: paper_position may already contain realized P&L from a Near-TP partial
397:         // harvest. Preserve it when the remaining quantity is later closed.
398:         BigDecimal pnl = (paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl())
399:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
400:         paper.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
401:         paper.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
402:         paper.setStatus(status);
403:         paper.setCloseReason(closeReason);
404:         paper.setExitReason(explanation);
405:         paper.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
406:         PaperPosition saved = paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
407: 
408:         // FIX-028: live-price exits (TP/SL/Profit Lock/normal exit) also write the
409:         // immutable audit record. sourceSignal is context only unless the closeTrigger
410:         // itself is a genuine SELL decision. No protection/exit policy is changed here.
411:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, sourceSignal, closeReason, explanation);
412:     }
413: }

59:         catch(RuntimeException failure){

64:         }

66:         String rejection=priceEvents.invalidReason(price,observedAt,exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow));
67:         if(rejection==null && priceEvents.laterApplied(symbol,observedAt))rejection="OUT_OF_ORDER";
68:         if(rejection!=null){priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"NON_CANONICAL",rejection);return;}
69:         onPrice(symbol,price);
70:         // No-position and all successful no-op exits reach this statement; exceptions do not.
71:         priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"APPLIED","PROTECTION_CALLBACK_COMPLETED");
72:     }

74:     @Transactional
75:     public void onPrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal price) {
76:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) return;
77:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
78: 
79:         // FIX-124: classify ONLY the first lock failure. The caller may retry after
80:         // rollback in a fresh transaction; failures after evaluation are not retried.
81:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
82:         WalletManagedPosition managed;
83:         try {
84:             managed = managedPositionRepository
85:                     .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
86:                     .orElse(null);
87:         } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
88:             if (com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure)) {
89:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
90:             }
91:             throw failure;
92:         }
93:         if (managed == null || managed.getQuantity() == null || managed.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) return;
94: 
95:         TradeSignal one = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "1m").orElse(null);
96:         TradeSignal five = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "5m").orElse(null);
97:         TradeSignal hour = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "1h").orElse(null);
98: 
99:         // Reaching TP is a management checkpoint, not an unconditional full exit.
100:         if (managed.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
101:             // Pass the complete immutable BUY thesis. PositionContinuationPolicy and
102:             // PositionManagementService now evaluate the same deterioration pressure (FIX-011).
103:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(one, five, hour,
104:                     managed.getEntryTrendScore(), managed.getEntryStructureScore(),
105:                     managed.getEntryMomentumScore(), managed.getEntryVolumeScore(),
106:                     managed.getEntryConfidence(), managed.getEntryTotalScore());
107:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
108:                 BigDecimal distance = managed.getTakeProfitUsdt().subtract(managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
109:                 BigDecimal oldTarget = managed.getTakeProfitUsdt();
110:                 BigDecimal newTarget = oldTarget.add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
111:                 Instant changedAt = Instant.now();
112: 
113:                 // FIX-118 behavioral phase: if Profit Lock was already earned under the old TP
114:                 // but the wider approved target moves current progress back below activation,

116:                 // lock remains stored but is non-executable until new-geometry activation is
117:                 // earned again.
118:                 DynamicProfitLockService.ExtensionTransition fix118Transition =
119:                         dynamicProfitLockService.onTakeProfitExtended(managed, newTarget, price, changedAt);
120:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][TP_EXTENSION] positionId={}, symbol={}, marketPrice={}, entry={}, oldTp={}, newTp={}, " +
121:                                 "highest={}, profitLockActive={}, profitLockPrice={}, previousState={}, state={}, " +
122:                                 "newGeometryProgressPct={}, activationPct={}",

124:                         managed.getHighestPriceUsdt(), managed.isProfitLockActive(), managed.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),

130:                 // arm/rejection was relative to the superseded target and must be restarted.
131:                 resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(managed);

package com.crypto.domain;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

@Entity
@Table(name = "paper_position")
@Getter @Setter @Builder @NoArgsConstructor @AllArgsConstructor
public class PaperPosition {
    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String symbol;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private PositionSide side;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private PositionStatus status;

    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal quantity;

    @Column(name = "entry_price", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal entryPrice;

    @Column(name = "stop_loss", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal stopLoss;

    @Column(name = "take_profit", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal takeProfit;

    @Column(name = "exit_price", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal exitPrice;

    @Column(name = "realized_pnl", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal realizedPnl;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "signal_id")
    private TradeSignal signal;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "exit_signal_id")
    private TradeSignal exitSignal;

    @Column(name = "close_reason", length = 40)
    private String closeReason;

    // FIX-144: Full signal/execution explanations and progressive additions exceed 2,000 chars.
    // Preserve audit text without truncation; V100 widens the existing physical columns.
    @Column(name = "entry_reason", columnDefinition = "MEDIUMTEXT")
    private String entryReason;

    // FIX-144: Exit audit explanations use the same expanded storage contract.
    @Column(name = "exit_reason", columnDefinition = "MEDIUMTEXT")
    private String exitReason;

    @Column(name = "opened_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant openedAt;

    @Column(name = "closed_at")
    private Instant closedAt;
}

package com.crypto.audit.domain;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

/**
 * FIX-028 diagnostic/audit record for one completed production position.
 *
 * This entity deliberately separates the terminal close trigger (TAKE_PROFIT,
 * STOP_LOSS, genuine SELL signal, Profit Lock, etc.) from the latest market
 * signal used as context at that moment. It is read-only evidence for debugging;
 * it never participates in trading decisions or execution authority.
 */
@Entity
@Table(name = "production_exit_audit")
@Getter
@Setter
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
public class ProductionExitAudit {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "paper_position_id", unique = true)
    private Long paperPositionId;

    @Column(name = "wallet_position_id")
    private Long walletPositionId;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String symbol;

    @Column(name = "close_trigger", nullable = false, length = 40)
    private String closeTrigger;

    @Column(name = "source_signal_id")
    private Long sourceSignalId;

    @Column(name = "source_signal_decision", length = 30)
    private String sourceSignalDecision;

    @Column(name = "source_signal_original_decision", length = 30)
    private String sourceSignalOriginalDecision;

    @Column(name = "position_analysis_id")
    private Long positionAnalysisId;

    @Column(name = "position_recommendation", length = 20)
    private String positionRecommendation;

    @Column(name = "entry_price_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal entryPriceUsdt;

    @Column(name = "exit_price_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal exitPriceUsdt;

    @Column(name = "stop_loss_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal stopLossUsdt;

    @Column(name = "take_profit_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal takeProfitUsdt;

    @Column(name = "close_explanation", length = 2000)
    private String closeExplanation;

    @Column(name = "audited_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant auditedAt;
}

package com.crypto.wallet.domain;

public class WalletTrade {

    private Long id;
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "signal_id")
    private TradeSignal signal;

    private PositionAnalysis positionAnalysis;

    private String executionKey;

    private String symbol;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private String side;
    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal quantity;
    @Column(name = "price_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal priceUsdt;
    /** FIX-056 immutable signal-time price used for decision audit only. */
    @Column(name = "decision_price_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal decisionPriceUsdt;
    /** FIX-056 UTC timestamp of the canonical market event used as the fill authority. */
    @Column(name = "execution_price_observed_at")
    private Instant executionPriceObservedAt;
    @Column(name = "gross_amount_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal grossAmountUsdt;
    @Column(name = "fee_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal feeUsdt;
    @Column(name = "net_amount_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal netAmountUsdt;
    @Column(name = "cost_basis_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal costBasisUsdt;
    @Column(name = "realized_pnl_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal realizedPnlUsdt;
    @Column(name = "realized_pnl_percent", precision = 20, scale = 8)
    private BigDecimal realizedPnlPercent;
    @Column(name = "execution_type", nullable = false, length = 20)
    private String executionType;
    @Column(name = "execution_reason", length = 40)
    private String executionReason;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private String status;
    @Column(name = "executed_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant executedAt;
    @Column(length = 500)
    private String notes;
    // FIX-126: complete execution explanations must not abort wallet persistence.
    @Column(name = "execution_message", columnDefinition = "MEDIUMTEXT")
    private String executionMessage;
}

package com.crypto.market.service;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * FIX-052 exact Replay/Production parity support.
 *
 * Production protects open positions from every canonical 1m Binance live-price
 * update, not only from candle-close analysis signals. Persisting that exact feed
 * lets Replay evaluate TP/SL/profit-lock against the same observations and order.
 * Timestamps are always stored/interpreted as UTC Instants; timezone conversion is
 * a presentation concern only.
 */
@Service
@org.springframework.context.annotation.DependsOn("localTraderInstance")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class MarketPriceEventService {
    private static final int PRICE_SCALE=12;
    private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;

    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode="OFF";
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${local-candle.price-max-age-seconds:15}") private long maxAge=15;
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${local-candle.price-future-skew-seconds:2}") private long futureSkew=2;
    private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(MarketPriceEventService.class);
    private final java.util.Set<Long> inFlight=java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    public static final String DIRECT_SOURCE="BINANCE_KLINE_DIRECT_FIX153";
    public static BigDecimal normalizePrice(BigDecimal price){
        return price==null?null:price.setScale(PRICE_SCALE,java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }
    private Instant referenceNow(){Instant local=Instant.now();return exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);}
    public String invalidReason(BigDecimal price,Instant observed,Instant now){
        if(price==null || price.signum()<=0)return "INVALID_PRICE";
        if(observed==null)return "MISSING_TIMESTAMP";
        if(observed.isAfter(now.plusSeconds(futureSkew)))return "FUTURE_TIMESTAMP";
        if(observed.isBefore(now.minusSeconds(maxAge)))return "STALE_PRICE";
        return null;
    }
    /** FIX-153: called in INPUT transaction. Invalid observations never occupy slot 1.
     * A duplicate-key error is classified only if the exact canonical key exists; other
     * duplicate constraints/errors propagate. JDBC statement errors do not poison a JPA proxy TX. */
    public Long recordPending(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observed){
        BigDecimal normalizedPrice=normalizePrice(price);
        Instant now=referenceNow();String invalid=invalidReason(normalizedPrice,observed,now);
        if(invalid!=null){
            com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
            Long ageMs=observed==null?null:java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis();
            log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_NOT_CANONICAL] symbol={}, price={}, observedUtc={}, observationAgeMs={}, reason={}",symbol,normalizedPrice,observed,ageMs,invalid);
            return null;
        }
        org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder keys=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
        try {
            jdbcTemplate.update(connection->{
                var ps=connection.prepareStatement("INSERT INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,delivery_status,ordering_slot) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'PENDING',1)",new String[]{"id"});
                ps.setString(1,symbol);ps.setTimestamp(2,Timestamp.from(observed));ps.setBigDecimal(3,normalizedPrice);ps.setString(4,DIRECT_SOURCE);return ps;
            },keys);
            long id=java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(keys.getKey(),"generated price id").longValue();inFlight.add(id);return id;
        }catch(org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate){
            var first=jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id,price FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND ordering_slot=1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed));
            if(first.size()!=1)throw duplicate;
            BigDecimal existing=(BigDecimal)first.getFirst().get("price");
            log.warn("[FIX-153][EQUAL_TIMESTAMP_REJECTED] symbol={}, observedUtc={}, firstEvent={}, firstPrice={}, rejectedPrice={}, observationAgeMs={}, priceDisagreement={}",symbol,observed,first.getFirst().get("id"),existing,normalizedPrice,java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis(),existing.compareTo(normalizedPrice)!=0);
            jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,delivery_status,ordering_slot,outcome_reason) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'NON_CANONICAL',NULL,?)",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),normalizedPrice,DIRECT_SOURCE,existing.compareTo(normalizedPrice)==0?"DUPLICATE_TIMESTAMP":"AMBIGUOUS_EQUAL_TIMESTAMP");
            com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);return null;
        }
    }
    public void release(long id){inFlight.remove(id);}
    /** Runs after existing symbol/position lock at READ_COMMITTED, never counts unresolved receipts. */
    public boolean laterApplied(String symbol,Instant observed){
        return !jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at>? AND source IN ('BINANCE_KLINE_LIVE_CLOSE',?) AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') ORDER BY observed_at DESC LIMIT 1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),DIRECT_SOURCE).isEmpty();
    }
    /** Exact event lineage prevents a caller from completing a different receipt.
     * A lost resolver race rolls back the entire effects transaction. Slot 1 is retained. */
    public void finish(long id,String symbol,Instant observed,BigDecimal price,String state,String reason){
        price=normalizePrice(price);
        if(!org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
            throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome requires effects transaction");
        if(!java.util.Set.of("APPLIED","NON_CANONICAL").contains(state))throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 invalid effects outcome");
        int n=jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=?,outcome_reason=? WHERE id=? AND source=? AND symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND price=? AND ordering_slot=1 AND delivery_status='PENDING'",state,reason,id,DIRECT_SOURCE,symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),price);
        if(n!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome ownership/lineage changed: event="+id);
        org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization(){
            @Override public void afterCompletion(int status){
                if(status==STATUS_COMMITTED){
                    com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count("APPLIED".equals(state)?com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_APPLIED:com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
                    if("APPLIED".equals(state))com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.protection(symbol,observed);
                    log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_OUTCOME_COMMITTED] event={}, symbol={}, observedUtc={}, observedKsa={}, outcome={}, reason={}",id,symbol,observed,observed.atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)),state,reason);
                }else log.debug("[FIX-153][PRICE_OUTCOME_NOT_COMMITTED] event={}, symbol={}, proposedOutcome={}, transactionCompletion={}; durable outcome must be read",id,symbol,state,status);
            }
        });
    }
    /** Startup and periodic conditional resolution: no replay and no symbol-entry fence.
     * The conditional update waits behind an in-flight effect TX and cannot override APPLIED.
     * Runtime absence alone is insufficient; candidates must also exceed a configured bound. */
    @org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled(initialDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-initial-delay-ms:5000}",fixedDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-delay-ms:5000}")
    public void resolvePending(){
        if(!"OFF".equals(mode))return;
        try {
            // FIX-153: housekeeping uses its own statement timeout, without mutating the shared template.
            var bounded=new JdbcTemplate(java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(jdbcTemplate.getDataSource()));bounded.setQueryTimeout(2);
            long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
            var rows=bounded.queryForList("SELECT id,symbol,observed_at FROM market_price_event WHERE source=? AND delivery_status='PENDING' AND created_at<? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 100",DIRECT_SOURCE,Timestamp.from(Instant.now().minusSeconds(120)));
            for(var row:rows){if(System.nanoTime()>deadline)break;long id=((Number)row.get("id")).longValue();if(inFlight.contains(id))continue;
                int n=bounded.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status='PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED',outcome_reason='AUTOMATIC_ATOMIC_NO_COMMIT' WHERE id=? AND source=? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",id,DIRECT_SOURCE);
                if(n==1){com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_PENDING_RESOLVED);
                    log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_RESOLVED] event={}, symbol={}, observedUtc={}, outcome=PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED, evidence=ATOMIC_EFFECTS_AND_STATUS_NO_COMMIT, replay=false",id,row.get("symbol"),row.get("observed_at"));}
            }
        }catch(RuntimeException failure){log.warn("[FIX-153][PENDING_RESOLUTION_FAILED] durable outcome unchanged; no live fence introduced",failure);}
    }

    public List<PriceEvent> find(String rawSymbol, Instant startInclusive, Instant endInclusive) {
        String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        return jdbcTemplate.query("""
                SELECT observed_at, price, source
                FROM market_price_event
                WHERE delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') AND symbol = ? AND observed_at >= ? AND observed_at <= ?
                ORDER BY observed_at ASC, id ASC
                """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PriceEvent(
                        rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
                        rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),
                symbol, Timestamp.from(startInclusive), Timestamp.from(endInclusive));
    }


    /** FIX-056: newest canonical live-price observation at or before the execution clock. */
    public java.util.Optional<PriceEvent> findLatestAtOrBefore(String rawSymbol, Instant reference) {
        String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        List<PriceEvent> rows = jdbcTemplate.query("""
                SELECT observed_at, price, source
                FROM market_price_event
                WHERE delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') AND symbol = ? AND observed_at <= ?
                ORDER BY observed_at DESC, id DESC
                LIMIT 1
                """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PriceEvent(
                        rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
                        rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),
                symbol, Timestamp.from(reference));
        return rows.stream().findFirst();
    }

    /** FIX-132: known skipped/unconfirmed collector delivery must not silently
     * become legacy candle-price fallback. Historical pre-collector windows retain
     * their established fallback. This reads immutable evidence, never live claims. */
    public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
        Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",
            Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
        if(pending!=null && pending>0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 Replay contains unconfirmed Production price outcomes; reconcile before claiming parity");
        // FIX-153 OFF Replay never queries collector-era activation tables.
        if("OFF".equals(mode))return;
        Long cutovers=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at<=?",Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(to));
        if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {
            Long applied=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED')",
                Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
            if(applied==null || applied==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 no applied price evidence in collector-era Replay window; signal-price fallback is not Production reproduction");
        }
    }

    /** FIX-153: report excluded evidence explicitly; this never promotes it to a Replay price. */
    public java.util.Map<String,Long> replayOutcomeCounts(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to){
        java.util.Map<String,Long> result=new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
        jdbcTemplate.query("SELECT delivery_status,COUNT(*) AS n FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? GROUP BY delivery_status",
            (org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowCallbackHandler)rs->result.put(rs.getString(1),rs.getLong(2)),symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
        return result;
    }

    public record PriceEvent(Instant observedAt, BigDecimal price, String source) {
        public PriceEvent(Instant observedAt,BigDecimal price) { this(observedAt,price,"BINANCE_KLINE_LIVE_CLOSE"); }
    }
}

## FINDINGS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** No executed result exists in the permitted `db/CODEX` folder. Therefore none of the six frozen losses—`104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78`—can yet be assigned a trade-specific root cause.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE — trade 104 candidate]** The logs previously identified a live EDUUSDT profit-lock breach at UTC `2026-10-10 14:45:22.035` / KSA `17:45:22.035`, held by the live route under `1h=BUY, 5m=WATCH, 1m=WATCH`. A 1m signal route then logged a profit-lock trigger at UTC `14:46:01.312` / KSA `17:46:01.312`. The observed interval is ~39.3 seconds.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The two paths have materially different rules:
  - Live protection calls `PositionExitPolicy`; it holds a breached profit lock with bullish 1h plus supportive 5m. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:219) [PositionExitPolicy.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionExitPolicy.java:57)
  - Signal processing closes immediately when its own profit-lock evaluation is triggered; it does not call that policy. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:176)

- **[AGENT INFERENCE — trade 104]** This is a credible exit-route divergence candidate, not yet a confirmed cause of position `104`’s loss. The database must prove that these log events map to `paper_position.id=104`, identify the wallet position and audit trigger, and reconcile any partial realization.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** There is also a confirmed consistency risk in the signal exit route: it persists the paper close before attempting the wallet exit, then catches a wallet exception. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:501) [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:515) The live route performs the wallet mechanical exit first and closes paper state only on success. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:165) This is not evidence that it occurred in any frozen trade.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Paper P&L retains any earlier realized component when the remainder is closed. Thus, a final exit price above the initial paper entry does not alone disprove a negative final `realized_pnl`; partial-harvest and wallet records are required. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:457)

- **[EXTERNAL RESEARCH]** OHLC candles cannot establish the order of intrabar stop/target/lock contacts; price-event ordering is needed. This reinforces the project rule not to use candle high/low as a substitute for accepted protection evidence. [Research summary](https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=6240638)

## UNKNOWNS

- Exact entry/exit linkage, trigger, P&L, fees, partial fills, and price-event chain for every frozen ID.
- Whether trade `104`’s route divergence changed its realized outcome.
- Whether any of `101, 96, 91, 82, 78` used signal-path versus live-protection closure.
- The active historical configuration/version and whether FIX-154 behavior was active at each entry.
- Snapshot metadata remains internally inconsistent: UTC `15:00:04` converts to KSA `18:00:04`, while supplied snapshot KSA is `18:00:02`.

### DB_REQUEST
QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I6-001  
Purpose: Establish the bounded, durable root-cause chain for exactly the six frozen positions: authoritative lifecycle, immutable exit audit, managed position, related signals, wallet fills, and accepted/rejected price evidence around each close. Raw timestamps must be interpreted as UTC; KSA is UTC+03:00.

SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    @@session.time_zone AS session_time_zone,
    @@global.time_zone AS global_time_zone,
    @@system_time_zone AS system_time_zone;

SELECT
    table_name,
    ordinal_position,
    column_name,
    column_type,
    is_nullable,
    column_key
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'crypto_ai'
  AND table_name IN (
      'paper_position',
      'production_exit_audit',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'wallet_trade',
      'trade_signal',
      'market_price_event'
  )
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position;

SELECT
    p.*,
    pea.id AS exit_audit_id,
    pea.wallet_position_id,
    pea.close_trigger AS audit_close_trigger,
    pea.source_signal_id AS audit_source_signal_id,
    pea.source_signal_decision AS audit_source_signal_decision,
    pea.entry_price_usdt AS audit_entry_price_usdt,
    pea.exit_price_usdt AS audit_exit_price_usdt,
    pea.close_explanation AS audit_close_explanation,
    pea.audited_at
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
LEFT JOIN crypto_ai.production_exit_audit pea
  ON pea.paper_position_id = p.id
WHERE p.id IN (104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78)
ORDER BY FIELD(p.id, 104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78);

SELECT wmp.*
FROM crypto_ai.wallet_managed_position wmp
JOIN crypto_ai.production_exit_audit pea
  ON pea.wallet_position_id = wmp.id
WHERE pea.paper_position_id IN (104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78)
ORDER BY pea.paper_position_id, wmp.id;

SELECT ts.*
FROM crypto_ai.trade_signal ts
WHERE ts.id IN (
    SELECT signal_id
    FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
    WHERE id IN (104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78)
    UNION
    SELECT exit_signal_id
    FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
    WHERE id IN (104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78)
    UNION
    SELECT source_signal_id
    FROM crypto_ai.production_exit_audit
    WHERE paper_position_id IN (104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78)
)
ORDER BY ts.generated_at, ts.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    wt.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.wallet_trade wt
  ON wt.symbol = p.symbol
 AND wt.executed_at >= p.opened_at - INTERVAL 1 MINUTE
 AND wt.executed_at <= p.closed_at + INTERVAL 1 MINUTE
WHERE p.id IN (104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78)
ORDER BY p.id, wt.executed_at, wt.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    mpe.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.market_price_event mpe
  ON mpe.symbol = p.symbol
 AND mpe.observed_at >= p.closed_at - INTERVAL 2 MINUTE
 AND mpe.observed_at <= p.closed_at + INTERVAL 15 SECOND
WHERE p.id IN (104, 101, 96, 91, 82, 78)
ORDER BY p.id, mpe.observed_at, mpe.id;
```
